Theorem 219.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The computations variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 236 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 10 and intersection form of rank 5.

The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^10(S, F) is trivial. QED.
